		tooltip_info = intrigue
		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = deceitful } } add_trait = deceitful }
		random_list = {
			50 = {
				modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = craven } }
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { trait = trusting } }
				modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = slow } }
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { trait = imbecile } }
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { trait = inbred } }
				modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = weak } }
				modifier = { factor = 0.75 FROM = { trait = paranoid } }
				modifier = { factor = 0.75 FROM = { trait = quick } }
				modifier = { factor = 0.5 FROM = { trait = genius } }
				modifier = { factor = 0.75 FROM = { trait = shrewd } }
				modifier = { factor = 0.75 FROM = { trait = brave } }
				modifier = { factor = 0.75 FROM = { trait = strong } }
				modifier = { factor = 0.75 FROM = { trait = duelist } }

				modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = schemer }
				modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = quick }
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = genius }
				modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = shrewd }
				modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = strong }
				modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = duelist }
				modifier = {
					factor = 1.25
					attribute_diff = { character = FROM attribute = intrigue value = 2 }
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 1.25
					attribute_diff = { character = FROM attribute = intrigue value = 4 }
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 1.25
					attribute_diff = { character = FROM attribute = intrigue value = 6 }
				}
				modifier = {

					attribute_diff = { character = FROM attribute = intrigue value = 10 }
				}

			modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = brave }
			modifier = { factor = 0 trait = just }
			modifier = { factor = 0 trait = kind }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBsway144
		opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_swayed_3 years = 10 }
		FROM = {
			show_scope_change = no
			opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_swayed_3 years = 10 }
		}

	}
}

#On_death: Clean up event for outliner characters.
character_event = {
	id = sway.152
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { persistent_event_target:antagonize_target = { always = yes } }
			remove_special_interest = persistent_event_target:antagonize_target
		}
		if = {
			limit = { persistent_event_target:sway_target = { always = yes } }
			remove_special_interest = persistent_event_target:sway_target
			clr_character_flag = flag_tired_of_gifts
		}
	}
}

		text = EVTDESCsway200_STEWARDSHIP
	}

	desc = { trigger = { has_education_learning_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCsway200_LEARNING }

	picture = GFX_evt_woman_at_window_hf
	portrait = persistent_event_target:sway_target
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { #Try to help out
		name = {
			trigger = { has_education_diplomacy_trigger = yes }
			text = EVTOPTAsway200_DIPLOMACY
		}

		name = { trigger = { has_education_martial_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTAsway200_MARTIAL }

		name = {
			trigger = { has_education_stewardship_trigger = yes }
			text = EVTOPTAsway200_STEWARDSHIP
		}

		name = { trigger = { has_education_intrigue_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTAsway200_INTRIGUE }

		name = { trigger = { has_education_learning_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTAsway200_LEARNING }

		if = {
			limit = { has_education_diplomacy_trigger = yes }
			custom_tooltip = { text = EVTTOOLTIPsway200_DIPLOMACY }
		}

		if = {
			limit = { has_education_martial_trigger = yes }
			custom_tooltip = { text = EVTTOOLTIPsway200_MARTIAL }
		}

		if = {
			limit = { has_education_stewardship_trigger = yes }
			custom_tooltip = { text = EVTTOOLTIPsway200_STEWARDSHIP }
		}

		if = {
			limit = { has_education_intrigue_trigger = yes }
			custom_tooltip = { text = EVTTOOLTIPsway200_INTRIGUE }
		}

		if = {
			limit = { has_education_learning_trigger = yes }
			custom_tooltip = { text = EVTTOOLTIPsway200_LEARNING }
		}

		persistent_event_target:sway_target = { character_event = { id = sway.201 } }

		ai_chance = { factor = 80 }
	}
	option = { #Refrain from helping
		name = EVTOPTBsway200
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTTOOLTIPsway200_NO }

		ai_chance = { factor = 20 }
	}
}

			50 = {

				add_character_modifier = { name = confused_by_diplomacy duration = 3650 }
			}
			50 = {
				trigger = { has_education_martial_trigger = yes }
				add_character_modifier = { name = confused_by_warfare duration = 3650 }
			}
			50 = {
				trigger = { has_education_stewardship_trigger = yes }
				add_character_modifier = { name = confused_by_governing duration = 3650 }
			}
			50 = {
				trigger = { has_education_intrigue_trigger = yes }
				add_character_modifier = { name = bad_image duration = 3650 }
			}
			50 = {
				trigger = { has_education_learning_trigger = yes }
				add_character_modifier = { name = terrible_writer duration = 3650 }
			}
			50 = {
				trigger = { has_education_martial_trigger = yes }
				add_character_modifier = { name = unrespected_general duration = 3650 }
			}
			50 = {
				trigger = { has_education_intrigue_trigger = yes }
				add_character_modifier = { name = unpopular_image duration = 3650 }
			}
			50 = {
				trigger = {
					OR = {
						has_education_diplomacy_trigger = yes
						has_education_learning_trigger = yes
						has_education_stewardship_trigger = yes
					}
				}
				add_character_modifier = { name = jumbling_words duration = 3650 }
			}
		}
	}

		FROMFROMFROM = { is_alive = yes }

		name = EVTOPTAsway206

		else_if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = humble } } add_trait = humble }

		name = EVTOPTBsway206

		FROMFROMFROM = { character_event = { id = sway.207 } }
	}
}

#Things went neutral - Swayee / Sway Target
character_event = {
	id = sway.208

	desc = { trigger = { has_education_diplomacy_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCsway208_DIPLOMACY }

	desc = { trigger = { has_education_martial_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCsway208_MARTIAL }

	desc = {
		trigger = { has_education_stewardship_trigger = yes }
		text = EVTDESCsway208_STEWARDSHIP
	}

	desc = { trigger = { has_education_intrigue_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCsway208_INTRIGUE }

	desc = { trigger = { has_education_learning_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCsway208_LEARNING }

	portrait = FROMFROM
	picture = GFX_evt_relaxed_ruler
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		FROMFROM = { is_alive = yes }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAsway208
		character_event = { id = sway.203 days = 15 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_education_diplomacy_trigger = yes
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = well_spoken }
			}
			add_character_modifier = { name = well_spoken duration = 3650 }
		}
		else_if = { limit = { has_education_diplomacy_trigger = yes } change_diplomacy = 1 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_education_martial_trigger = yes
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = well_spoken }
			}
			add_character_modifier = { name = well_drilled duration = 3650 }
		}
		else_if = { limit = { has_education_martial_trigger = yes } change_martial = 1 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_education_stewardship_trigger = yes
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = well_governed }
			}
			add_character_modifier = { name = well_governed duration = 3650 }
		}
		else_if = { limit = { has_education_stewardship_trigger = yes } change_stewardship = 1 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_education_intrigue_trigger = yes
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = good_image }
			}
			add_character_modifier = { name = good_image duration = 3650 }
		}
		else_if = { limit = { has_education_intrigue_trigger = yes } change_intrigue = 1 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_education_learning_trigger = yes
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = well_written }
			}
			add_character_modifier = { name = well_written duration = 3650 }
		}
		else_if = { limit = { has_education_intrigue_trigger = yes } change_intrigue = 1 }
	}
}

# Invite the Sway Target to lifestyle event - Root / Swayer
character_event = {
	id = sway.220
	desc = EVTDESCsway220

	picture = GFX_evt_throne_room
	portrait = persistent_event_target:sway_target
	is_triggered_only = yes
	portrait = persistent_event_target:sway_target

	trigger = {
		persistent_event_target:sway_target = { is_alive = yes }
	}
	immediate = {
		save_event_target_as = swayer_character

		reset_flag_lifestyle_sway_event_effect = yes

		persistent_event_target:sway_target = { set_character_flag = lifestyle_sway_event }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAsway220_HUNT
		trigger = { trait = hunter }
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAsway220_TT_HUNT }
		set_character_flag = sway_hunting

		persistent_event_target:sway_target = { character_Event = { id = sway.221 days = 10 } }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAsway220_DUELIST
		trigger = { trait = duelist }
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAsway220_TT_DUEL }
		set_character_flag = sway_dueling

		persistent_event_target:sway_target = { character_Event = { id = sway.221 days = 10 } }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAsway220_GAME
		trigger = { OR = { trait = gamer trait = strategist } }
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAsway220_TT_GAME }
		set_character_flag = sway_gamer

		persistent_event_target:sway_target = { character_Event = { id = sway.221 days = 10 } }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAsway220_SOCIAL
		trigger = {
			OR = { trait = socializer trait = seducer trait = seductress trait = hedonist }
		}
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAsway220_TT_SOCIAL }
		set_character_flag = sway_social

		persistent_event_target:sway_target = { character_Event = { id = sway.221 days = 10 } }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAsway220_SCHOLAR
		trigger = { OR = { trait = mystic trait = faqih trait = scholar trait = theologian } }
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAsway220_TT_SCHOLAR }
		set_character_flag = sway_scholar

		persistent_event_target:sway_target = { character_Event = { id = sway.221 days = 10 } }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAsway220_STEWARD
		trigger = { OR = { trait = administrator trait = gardener trait = architect } }
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAsway220_TT_STEWARD }
		set_character_flag = sway_steward

		persistent_event_target:sway_target = { character_Event = { id = sway.221 days = 10 } }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAsway220_TORTURE
		trigger = { OR = { trait = impaler trait = schemer } }
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAsway220_TT_TORTURE }
		set_character_flag = sway_torture

		persistent_event_target:sway_target = { character_Event = { id = sway.221 days = 10 } }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAsway220_HUNT
		trigger = { NOT = { trait = hunter } martial = 8 }
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAsway220_TT_HUNT }
		set_character_flag = sway_hunt

		persistent_event_target:sway_target = { character_Event = { id = sway.221 days = 10 } }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAsway220_SOCIAL
		trigger = {
			NOR = { trait = socializer trait = seducer trait = seductress trait = hedonist }
			OR = { diplomacy = 8 intrigue = 8 }
		}
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAsway220_TT_SOCIAL }
		set_character_flag = sway_social

		persistent_event_target:sway_target = { character_Event = { id = sway.221 days = 10 } }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAsway220_SCHOLAR
		trigger = {
			NOR = { trait = mystic trait = faqih trait = scholar trait = theologian }
			learning = 8
		}
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAsway220_TT_SCHOLAR }
		set_character_flag = sway_scholar

		persistent_event_target:sway_target = { character_Event = { id = sway.221 days = 10 } }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAsway220_STEWARD
		trigger = {
			NOR = { trait = administrator trait = gardener trait = architect }
			stewardship = 8
		}
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTAsway220_TT_STEWARD }
		set_character_flag = sway_steward

		persistent_event_target:sway_target = { character_Event = { id = sway.221 days = 10 } }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBsway220
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTBsway220_TT }
	}
}

#Goal happens - Root / Swayer
character_event = {
	id = sway.225
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = sway_hunting }
		text = EVTDESCsway225_HUNT
		picture = GFX_evt_mysterious_forest
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = sway_dueling }
		text = EVTDESCsway225_DUEL
		picture = GFX_evt_family_feud_republic
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = sway_gamer }
		text = EVTDESCsway225_GAME
		picture = GFX_evt_war_planning
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = sway_social }
		text = EVTDESCsway225_SOCIAL
		picture = GFX_evt_dancing
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = sway_scholar }
		text = EVTDESCsway225_SCHOLAR
		picture = GFX_evt_council
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = sway_steward }
		text = EVTDESCsway225_STEWARD
		picture = GFX_evt_castle_garden
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = sway_torture }
		text = EVTDESCsway225_TORTURE
		picture = GFX_evt_into_the_dungeon
	}

	picture = GFX_evt_throne_room
	portrait = FROM
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		persistent_event_target:sway_target = { is_alive = yes }
	}

	fail_trigger_effect = { reset_flag_lifestyle_sway_event_effect = yes }

	option = {
		name = { trigger = { has_character_flag = sway_hunting } text = EVTOPTAsway225_HUNT }
		name = { trigger = { has_character_flag = sway_dueling } text = EVTOPTAsway225_DUEL }
		name = { trigger = { has_character_flag = sway_gamer } text = EVTOPTAsway225_GAME }
		name = { trigger = { has_character_flag = sway_social } text = EVTOPTAsway225_SOCIAL }
		name = { trigger = { has_character_flag = sway_scholar } text = EVTOPTAsway225_SCHOLAR }
		name = { trigger = { has_character_flag = sway_steward } text = EVTOPTAsway225_STEWARD }
		name = { trigger = { has_character_flag = sway_torture } text = EVTOPTAsway225_TORTURE }

		#Effects
		trigger_switch = {
			on_trigger = has_character_flag
			sway_hunting = {
				custom_tooltip = { text = EVTTOOLTIPAsway225_HUNT }
				if = { limit = { trait = patient } remove_trait = patient }
				else_if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = wroth } } add_trait = wroth }
			}
			sway_dueling = {
				custom_tooltip = { text = EVTTOOLTIPAsway225_DUEL }
				if = { limit = { trait = craven } remove_trait = craven }
				else_if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = brave } } add_trait = brave }
			}
			sway_gamer = {
				custom_tooltip = { text = EVTTOOLTIPAsway225_GAME }
				if = { limit = { trait = envious } remove_trait = envious }
				else_if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = kind } } add_trait = kind }
			}
			sway_social = {
				custom_tooltip = { text = EVTTOOLTIPAsway225_SOCIAL }
				if = { limit = { trait = chaste } remove_trait = chaste }
				else_if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = lustful } } add_trait = lustful }
			}
			sway_scholar = {
				custom_tooltip = { text = EVTTOOLTIPAsway225_SCHOLAR }
				if = { limit = { trait = slothful } remove_trait = slothful }
				else_if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = diligent } } add_trait = diligent }
			}
			sway_steward = {
				custom_tooltip = { text = EVTTOOLTIPAsway225_STEWARD }
				if = { limit = { trait = humble } remove_trait = humble }

			}
			sway_torture = {
				custom_tooltip = { text = EVTTOOLTIPAsway225_TORTURE }
				add_trait = cruel
			}
		}

		FROM = { character_event = { id = sway.226 days = 10 } }
	}
	option = {
		name = { trigger = { has_character_flag = sway_hunting } text = EVTOPTBsway225_HUNT }
		name = { trigger = { has_character_flag = sway_dueling } text = EVTOPTBsway225_DUEL }
		name = { trigger = { has_character_flag = sway_gamer } text = EVTOPTBsway225_GAME }
		name = { trigger = { has_character_flag = sway_social } text = EVTOPTBsway225_SOCIAL }
		name = { trigger = { has_character_flag = sway_scholar } text = EVTOPTBsway225_SCHOLAR }
		name = { trigger = { has_character_flag = sway_steward } text = EVTOPTBsway225_STEWARD }
		name = { trigger = { has_character_flag = sway_torture } text = EVTOPTBsway225_TORTURE }

			on_trigger = has_character_flag
			sway_hunting = {

				else_if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = patient } } add_trait = patient }
			}
			sway_dueling = {
				custom_tooltip = { text = EVTTOOLTIPBsway225_DUEL }
				if = { limit = { trait = wroth } remove_trait = wroth }
				else_if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = patient } } add_trait = patient }
			}
			sway_gamer = {
				custom_tooltip = { text = EVTTOOLTIPBsway225_GAME }
				if = { limit = { trait = content } remove_trait = content }
				else_if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = ambitious } } add_trait = ambitious }
			}
			sway_social = {
				custom_tooltip = { text = EVTTOOLTIPBsway225_SOCIAL }
				if = { limit = { trait = lustful } remove_trait = lustful }
				else_if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = chaste } } add_trait = chaste }
			}
			sway_scholar = {
				custom_tooltip = { text = EVTTOOLTIPBsway225_SCHOLAR }
				if = { limit = { trait = ambitious } remove_trait = ambitious }
				else_if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = content } } add_trait = content }
			}
			sway_steward = {
				custom_tooltip = { text = EVTTOOLTIPBsway225_STEWARD }
				persistent_event_target:sway_target = {
					opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_useless_host years = 5 }
				}
				if = { limit = { trait = deceitful } remove_trait = deceitful }
				else_if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = honest } } add_trait = honest }
			}
			sway_torture = {
				custom_tooltip = { text = EVTTOOLTIPBsway225_TORTURE }
				add_trait = cruel
			}
		}

		#Next event
		persistent_event_target:sway_target = { character_event = { id = sway.227 days = 10 } }
	}
}

		##Opinions
		#Hunter
		If = {
			limit = { event_target:swayer_character = { has_character_flag = sway_hunting } }
			event_target:swayer_character = {
				show_scope_change = no
				opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_weak_hunter years = 5 }
			}
		}
		#Dueling
		if = {
			limit = { event_target:swayer_character = { has_character_flag = sway_dueling } }
			event_target:swayer_character = {
				show_scope_change = no
				opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_weak_duelist years = 5 }
			}
		}
		#Game
		if = {
			limit = { event_target:swayer_character = { has_character_flag = sway_gamer } }
			event_target:swayer_character = {
				show_scope_change = no
				opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_fought_together years = 15 }
			}
		}
		#Scholar
		if = {
			limit = { event_target:swayer_character = { has_character_flag = sway_scholar } }
			opinion = {
				who = event_target:swayer_character
				modifier = opinion_impressive_speaker
				years = 5
			}
		}
		#Steward
		if = {
			limit = { event_target:swayer_character = { has_character_flag = sway_steward } }
			opinion = {
				who = event_target:swayer_character
				modifier = opinion_confident_host
				years = 5
			}
		}
		#Torturer
		if = {
			limit = { event_target:swayer_character = { has_character_flag = sway_torture } }
			opinion = {
				who = event_target:swayer_character
				modifier = opinion_impaler_friends
				years = 5
			}
			event_target:swayer_character = {
				show_scope_change = no
				opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_impaler_friends years = 5 }
			}
		}

		##Effects
		#Hunting /Dueling Effects
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:swayer_character = {
					OR = { has_character_flag = sway_hunting has_character_flag = sway_dueling }
				}
			}
			if = { limit = { trait = wroth } remove_trait = wroth }
			else_if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = patient } } add_trait = patient }
		}
		#Game
		if = {
			limit = { event_target:swayer_character = { has_character_flag = sway_gamer } }
			if = { limit = { trait = content } remove_trait = content }
			else_if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = ambitious } } add_trait = ambitious }
		}
		#Social
		if = {
			limit = { event_target:swayer_character = { has_character_flag = sway_social } }
			if = { limit = { trait = lustful } remove_trait = lustful }
			else_if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = chaste } } add_trait = chaste }
		}
		#Scholar
		if = {
			limit = { event_target:swayer_character = { has_character_flag = sway_scholar } }
			if = { limit = { trait = ambitious } remove_trait = ambitious }
			else_if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = content } } add_trait = content }
		}
		#Steward
		if = {
			limit = { event_target:swayer_character = { has_character_flag = sway_steward } }
			if = { limit = { trait = paranoid } remove_trait = paranoid }
			else_if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = trusting } } add_trait = trusting }
		}
		#Torture
		if = {
			limit = { event_target:swayer_character = { has_character_flag = sway_torture } }
			if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = cruel } } add_trait = cruel }
		}

		event_target:swayer_character = { character_event = { id = sway.228 days = 15 } }
	}
}

###############################################################################
# The voices of the races (sway.299 - sway.303)
# Rulers who do not keep a court, or who sway one who does not, use these
# methods instead of letters, art, poems, visits and invitations. Each voice
# (common/scripted_triggers/wh_sway_triggers.txt) has its own message, gift
# and deed, and the target answers in its own voice. A rare reward: the
# target lends a band of its own kind (earmark sway_ally_band).
###############################################################################

#Target: hidden ping from sway.990.
character_event = {
	id = sway.299
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		FROM = { character_event = { id = sway.300 } }
	}
}

#Swayer: how do I win this one over? FROM is the target.
character_event = {
	id = sway.300
	desc = { trigger = { sway_voice_court_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCsway300_court }
	desc = { trigger = { sway_voice_undead_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCsway300_undead }
	desc = { trigger = { sway_voice_greenskin_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCsway300_greenskin }
	desc = { trigger = { sway_voice_skaven_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCsway300_skaven }
	desc = { trigger = { sway_voice_ogre_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCsway300_ogre }
	desc = { trigger = { sway_voice_lizardmen_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCsway300_lizardmen }
	desc = { trigger = { sway_voice_warband_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCsway300_warband }
	desc = { trigger = { sway_voice_daemon_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCsway300_daemon }
	picture = GFX_evt_sway_hf

	trigger = {
		FROM = { is_alive = yes }
	}
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { #Message
		name = { text = EVTOPTAsway300_court trigger = { sway_voice_court_trigger = yes } }
		name = { text = EVTOPTAsway300_undead trigger = { sway_voice_undead_trigger = yes } }
		name = { text = EVTOPTAsway300_greenskin trigger = { sway_voice_greenskin_trigger = yes } }
		name = { text = EVTOPTAsway300_skaven trigger = { sway_voice_skaven_trigger = yes } }
		name = { text = EVTOPTAsway300_ogre trigger = { sway_voice_ogre_trigger = yes } }
		name = { text = EVTOPTAsway300_lizardmen trigger = { sway_voice_lizardmen_trigger = yes } }
		name = { text = EVTOPTAsway300_warband trigger = { sway_voice_warband_trigger = yes } }
		name = { text = EVTOPTAsway300_daemon trigger = { sway_voice_daemon_trigger = yes } }
		custom_tooltip = { text = tooltip_sway300_A }
		tooltip_info = diplomacy
		set_character_flag = sway_voice_msg
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = sway.301 } } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
	}
	option = { #Gift
		name = { text = EVTOPTBsway300_court trigger = { sway_voice_court_trigger = yes } }
		name = { text = EVTOPTBsway300_undead trigger = { sway_voice_undead_trigger = yes } }
		name = { text = EVTOPTBsway300_greenskin trigger = { sway_voice_greenskin_trigger = yes } }
		name = { text = EVTOPTBsway300_skaven trigger = { sway_voice_skaven_trigger = yes } }
		name = { text = EVTOPTBsway300_ogre trigger = { sway_voice_ogre_trigger = yes } }
		name = { text = EVTOPTBsway300_lizardmen trigger = { sway_voice_lizardmen_trigger = yes } }
		name = { text = EVTOPTBsway300_warband trigger = { sway_voice_warband_trigger = yes } }
		name = { text = EVTOPTBsway300_daemon trigger = { sway_voice_daemon_trigger = yes } }
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				AND = {
					OR = {
						sway_voice_court_trigger = yes
						sway_voice_lizardmen_trigger = yes
						sway_voice_ogre_trigger = yes
						sway_voice_skaven_trigger = yes
						sway_voice_undead_trigger = yes
					}
					wealth = 50
				}
				AND = {
					NOR = {
						sway_voice_court_trigger = yes
						sway_voice_lizardmen_trigger = yes
						sway_voice_ogre_trigger = yes
						sway_voice_skaven_trigger = yes
						sway_voice_undead_trigger = yes
					}
					prestige = 50
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					sway_voice_court_trigger = yes
					sway_voice_lizardmen_trigger = yes
					sway_voice_ogre_trigger = yes
					sway_voice_skaven_trigger = yes
					sway_voice_undead_trigger = yes
				}
			}
			wealth = -50
		}
		else = { prestige = -50 }
		custom_tooltip = { text = tooltip_sway300_B }
		set_character_flag = sway_voice_gift
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = sway.301 } } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 80 modifier = { factor = 0.25 trait = greedy } }
	}
	option = { #Deed
		%s
		custom_tooltip = { text = tooltip_sway300_C }
		tooltip_info = martial
		if = { limit = { sway_voice_ogre_trigger = yes } wealth = -25 }
		set_character_flag = sway_voice_deed
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = sway.301 } } }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 60
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = brave }
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = wroth }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = craven }
		}
	}
	option = { #Not now
		name = EVTOPTDsway300
		ai_chance = { factor = 0 }
	}
}

#Target: receives the message, gift or deed. FROM is the swayer.

#Swayer: the target liked it. FROM is the target.
character_event = {
	id = sway.302
	desc = { trigger = { sway_voice_court_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCsway302_court }
	desc = { trigger = { sway_voice_undead_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCsway302_undead }
	desc = { trigger = { sway_voice_greenskin_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCsway302_greenskin }
	desc = { trigger = { sway_voice_skaven_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCsway302_skaven }
	desc = { trigger = { sway_voice_ogre_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCsway302_ogre }
	desc = { trigger = { sway_voice_lizardmen_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCsway302_lizardmen }
	desc = { trigger = { sway_voice_warband_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCsway302_warband }
	desc = { trigger = { sway_voice_daemon_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCsway302_daemon }
	picture = GFX_evt_pleasing_letter

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAsway302
		tooltip = {
			FROM = {
				show_scope_change = no
				opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_swayed_1 years = 5 }
			}
		}
		if = { # Rare: the target lends a band of its own kind.
			limit = {
				is_ruler = yes
				is_landed = yes
				capital_scope = { always = yes }
				FROM = { is_ruler = yes is_landed = yes }
				NOT = { has_character_flag = sway_got_ally_band }
			}
			random = {
				chance = 5
				set_character_flag = sway_got_ally_band
				if = {
					limit = { FROM = { sway_voice_court_trigger = yes } }
					custom_tooltip = {
						text = tooltip_sway302_band_court
						capital_scope = {
							PREV = {
								spawn_unit = {
									province = PREV
									owner = PREV
									troops = { heavy_infantry = { 80 80 } archers = { 40 40 } }
									earmark = sway_ally_band
									maintenance_multiplier = 0.5
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = { FROM = { sway_voice_undead_trigger = yes } }
					custom_tooltip = {
						text = tooltip_sway302_band_undead
						capital_scope = {
							PREV = {
								spawn_unit = {
									province = PREV
									owner = PREV
									troops = { heavy_infantry = { 120 120 } }
									earmark = sway_ally_band
									maintenance_multiplier = 0.5
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = { FROM = { sway_voice_greenskin_trigger = yes } }
					custom_tooltip = {
						text = tooltip_sway302_band_greenskin
						capital_scope = {
							PREV = {
								spawn_unit = {
									province = PREV
									owner = PREV
									troops = {
										light_infantry = { 150 150 }
										heavy_infantry = { 50 50 }
									}
									earmark = sway_ally_band
									maintenance_multiplier = 0.5
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = { FROM = { sway_voice_skaven_trigger = yes } }
					custom_tooltip = {
						text = tooltip_sway302_band_skaven
						capital_scope = {
							PREV = {
								spawn_unit = {
									province = PREV
									owner = PREV
									troops = { light_infantry = { 200 200 } }
									earmark = sway_ally_band
									maintenance_multiplier = 0.5
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = { FROM = { sway_voice_ogre_trigger = yes } }
					custom_tooltip = {
						text = tooltip_sway302_band_ogre
						capital_scope = {
							PREV = {
								spawn_unit = {
									province = PREV
									owner = PREV
									troops = { heavy_infantry = { 80 80 } }
									earmark = sway_ally_band
									maintenance_multiplier = 0.5
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = { FROM = { sway_voice_lizardmen_trigger = yes } }
					custom_tooltip = {
						text = tooltip_sway302_band_lizardmen
						capital_scope = {
							PREV = {
								spawn_unit = {
									province = PREV
									owner = PREV
									troops = { heavy_infantry = { 100 100 } pikemen = { 50 50 } }
									earmark = sway_ally_band
									maintenance_multiplier = 0.5
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = { FROM = { sway_voice_warband_trigger = yes } }
					custom_tooltip = {
						text = tooltip_sway302_band_warband
						capital_scope = {
							PREV = {
								spawn_unit = {
									province = PREV
									owner = PREV
									troops = {
										light_infantry = { 80 80 }
										light_cavalry = { 60 60 }
									}
									earmark = sway_ally_band
									maintenance_multiplier = 0.5
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = { FROM = { sway_voice_daemon_trigger = yes } }
					custom_tooltip = {
						text = tooltip_sway302_band_daemon
						capital_scope = {
							PREV = {
								spawn_unit = {
									province = PREV
									owner = PREV
									troops = {
										light_infantry = { 150 150 }
										heavy_infantry = { 30 30 }
									}
									earmark = sway_ally_band
									maintenance_multiplier = 0.5
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		clr_character_flag = sway_voice_msg
		clr_character_flag = sway_voice_gift
		clr_character_flag = sway_voice_deed
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
	}
}

#Swayer: the target refused. FROM is the target.
character_event = {
	id = sway.303
	desc = { trigger = { sway_voice_court_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCsway303_court }
	desc = { trigger = { sway_voice_undead_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCsway303_undead }
	desc = { trigger = { sway_voice_greenskin_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCsway303_greenskin }
	desc = { trigger = { sway_voice_skaven_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCsway303_skaven }
	desc = { trigger = { sway_voice_ogre_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCsway303_ogre }
	desc = { trigger = { sway_voice_lizardmen_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCsway303_lizardmen }
	desc = { trigger = { sway_voice_warband_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCsway303_warband }
	desc = { trigger = { sway_voice_daemon_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCsway303_daemon }
	picture = GFX_evt_upsetting_letter

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAsway303
		if = { # A greenskin who lost the brawl.
			limit = { sway_voice_greenskin_trigger = yes has_character_flag = sway_voice_deed }
			random = { chance = 25 add_trait_wounded_effect = yes }
		}
		if = { # A Skaven whose spies were seen.
			limit = { sway_voice_skaven_trigger = yes has_character_flag = sway_voice_msg }
			FROM = {
				show_scope_change = no
				opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_sway_skaven_spied years = 5 }
			}
		}
		clr_character_flag = sway_voice_msg
		clr_character_flag = sway_voice_gift
		clr_character_flag = sway_voice_deed
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
	}
}
